#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <functional>
#include <limits>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <tuple>
#include <vector>

#include <krpc/decoder.hpp>
#include <krpc/encoder.hpp>
#include <krpc/error.hpp>
#include <krpc/event.hpp>
#include <krpc/hash.hpp>
#include <krpc/object.hpp>
#include <krpc/service.hpp>
#include <krpc/stream.hpp>

namespace krpc {
namespace services {

class KRPC : public Service {
 public:
  explicit KRPC(Client* client);

  // Class forward declarations
  class Expression;
  class Type;

  // Structure forward declarations
  struct ClientInfo;
  struct ServerSettings;
  struct ServerStatistics;
  struct ServerStatus;

  /**
   * A method was invoked where at least one of the passed arguments does not
   * meet the parameter specification of the method.
   */
  class ArgumentException : public std::invalid_argument {
   public:
    inline explicit ArgumentException(const std::string& msg) : std::invalid_argument(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A null reference was passed to a method that does not accept it as a valid argument.
   */
  class ArgumentNullException : public std::invalid_argument {
   public:
    inline explicit ArgumentNullException(const std::string& msg) : std::invalid_argument(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * The value of an argument is outside the allowable range of values as defined by the invoked method.
   */
  class ArgumentOutOfRangeException : public std::out_of_range {
   public:
    inline explicit ArgumentOutOfRangeException(const std::string& msg) : std::out_of_range(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * An integer was divided by zero.
   */
  class DivideByZeroException : public std::domain_error {
   public:
    inline explicit DivideByZeroException(const std::string& msg) : std::domain_error(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A general error, described by its message.
   */
  class Error : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit Error(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * An index was outside the bounds of a collection, string or array.
   */
  class IndexOutOfRangeException : public std::out_of_range {
   public:
    inline explicit IndexOutOfRangeException(const std::string& msg) : std::out_of_range(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A method call was made to a method that is invalid
   * given the current state of the object.
   */
  class InvalidOperationException : public std::logic_error {
   public:
    inline explicit InvalidOperationException(const std::string& msg) : std::logic_error(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A key was not found in a dictionary.
   */
  class KeyNotFoundException : public std::out_of_range {
   public:
    inline explicit KeyNotFoundException(const std::string& msg) : std::out_of_range(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A method was invoked that is not supported.
   */
  class NotSupportedException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit NotSupportedException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A value was null where its type does not allow one.
   */
  class NullReferenceException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit NullReferenceException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * The game object that a service object refers to no longer exists,
   * so the object cannot be used. For example, the part it represents
   * was destroyed, or the vessel it belongs to was recovered.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::ObjectNotLoadedException for a game object that exists but is not
   * loaded.
   */
  class ObjectDestroyedException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit ObjectDestroyedException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * The game object that a service object refers to exists, but is not loaded.
   * For example, the game is loading, or the vessel carrying the part is unloaded.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::ObjectDestroyedException for a game object that no longer exists.
   */
  class ObjectNotLoadedException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit ObjectNotLoadedException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * An arithmetic or conversion operation overflowed.
   */
  class OverflowException : public std::overflow_error {
   public:
    inline explicit OverflowException(const std::string& msg) : std::overflow_error(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * A stream was removed by the server, e.g. an event that has finished.
   */
  class StreamRemovedException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit StreamRemovedException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * An operation did not complete in the time allotted to it.
   */
  class TimeoutException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit TimeoutException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * The procedure is not available in the current game scene.
   */
  class UnsupportedSceneException : public ::krpc::RPCError {
   public:
    inline explicit UnsupportedSceneException(const std::string& msg) : ::krpc::RPCError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * The game scene. See krpc::services::KRPC::game_scene().
   */
  enum struct GameScene {
    /**
     * The game scene showing the Kerbal Space Center buildings.
     */
    space_center = 0,
    /**
     * The game scene showing a vessel in flight (or on the launchpad/runway).
     */
    flight = 1,
    /**
     * The tracking station.
     */
    tracking_station = 2,
    /**
     * The Vehicle Assembly Building.
     */
    editor_vab = 3,
    /**
     * The Space Plane Hangar.
     */
    editor_sph = 4,
    /**
     * The mission builder.
     */
    mission_builder = 5,
    /**
     * The astronaut complex. This is a pseudo-scene, shown when the
     * astronaut complex facility is open within the space center scene.
     */
    astronaut_complex = 6,
    /**
     * Mission control. This is a pseudo-scene, shown when the
     * mission control facility is open within the space center scene.
     */
    mission_control = 7,
    /**
     * Research and development. This is a pseudo-scene, shown when the
     * research and development facility is open within the space center scene.
     */
    research_and_development = 8,
    /**
     * The administration facility. This is a pseudo-scene, shown when the
     * administration facility is open within the space center scene.
     */
    administration = 9
  };

  /**
   * The kind of a type, with the values of the communication protocol's type codes.
   */
  enum struct TypeCode {
    /**
     * A double precision floating point number.
     */
    double_ = 1,
    /**
     * A single precision floating point number.
     */
    float_ = 2,
    /**
     * A signed 32-bit integer.
     */
    s_int32 = 3,
    /**
     * A signed 64-bit integer.
     */
    s_int64 = 4,
    /**
     * An unsigned 32-bit integer.
     */
    u_int32 = 5,
    /**
     * An unsigned 64-bit integer.
     */
    u_int64 = 6,
    /**
     * A boolean.
     */
    bool_ = 7,
    /**
     * A string.
     */
    string = 8,
    /**
     * A sequence of bytes.
     */
    bytes = 9,
    /**
     * A class defined by a service.
     */
    class_ = 100,
    /**
     * An enumeration defined by a service.
     */
    enumeration = 101,
    /**
     * A structure defined by a service.
     */
    struct_ = 102,
    /**
     * A tuple, with the types of its values in krpc::services::KRPC::Type::types().
     */
    tuple = 300,
    /**
     * A list, with the type of its values in krpc::services::KRPC::Type::types().
     */
    list = 301,
    /**
     * A set, with the type of its values in krpc::services::KRPC::Type::types().
     */
    set = 302,
    /**
     * A dictionary, with the types of its keys and values in krpc::services::KRPC::Type::types().
     */
    dictionary = 303
  };

  /**
   * Create an event from a server side function that evaluates to a boolean value.
   *
   * The event fires when the function becomes true, and again each time it becomes true
   * after being false. Client libraries call this procedure to implement events. Use the
   * events of your client library instead. Calling this directly while using a client
   * library has undefined behavior.
   */
  ::krpc::Event add_event(KRPC::Expression function);

  /**
   * Add a stream of the value a server side function computes on each update.
   *
   * Client libraries call this procedure to implement server side functions. Use the
   * server side functions of your client library instead. Calling this directly while
   * using a client library has undefined behavior.
   */
  krpc::schema::Stream add_function_stream(KRPC::Expression function);

  /**
   * Add a streaming request and return its identifier.
   *
   * Client libraries call this procedure to implement streams. Use the streams of your
   * client library instead. Calling this directly while using a client library has
   * undefined behavior.
   */
  krpc::schema::Stream add_stream(krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call);

  /**
   * Compile a server side function, so that running, streaming or adding it as an
   * event does not compile it.
   *
   * Client libraries call this procedure to implement server side functions. Use the
   * server side functions of your client library instead. Calling this directly while
   * using a client library has undefined behavior.
   */
  void compile_function(KRPC::Expression function);

  /**
   * Returns the identifier for the current client.
   */
  std::string get_client_id();

  /**
   * Returns the name of the current client.
   * This is an empty string if the client has no name.
   */
  std::string get_client_name();

  /**
   * Returns information on all services, procedures, classes, properties etc. provided by the server.
   * Can be used by client libraries to automatically create functionality such as stubs.
   */
  krpc::schema::Services get_services();

  /**
   * Returns the status of the server, with its version, settings and performance statistics.
   */
  KRPC::ServerStatus get_status();

  /**
   * Hold the game on its current physics tick until krpc::services::KRPC::release_tick() is called
   * or the hold times out. Every call made in between is executed before the game
   * advances, so a read, compute and write all happen in the state of one tick.
   *
   * The game renders no frame, takes no input and runs no physics while the tick is
   * held. Keep a hold as short as possible, and release it even when the code between
   * the two calls fails.
   *
   * A tick is held once, so this waits for the next tick when the current one has
   * already been released. One client holds the tick at a time, and only a client
   * making a call can hold it: a stream or an event cannot.
   *
   * A hold ends by itself when it times out, when the client disconnects, and when the
   * client calls a procedure that takes more than one tick to finish, such as staging
   * or warping. Stream updates are sent once the tick is released, so waiting for a
   * stream or an event inside a hold waits out the timeout.
   */
  void hold_tick();

  /**
   * Let the game move on from the tick being held and hold the one after it, so that a
   * program acts on consecutive physics ticks. Holds the soonest tick it can if no tick
   * is currently being held.
   *
   * A release and a hold made as two separate calls let the game advance between them.
   * This takes the next tick without that gap. Call it at the top of a loop that has to
   * act on every tick, work, and come back to it.
   *
   * The game advances one tick per call, so it runs at the rate of the loop for as long
   * as the loop runs. End the loop with krpc::services::KRPC::release_tick().
   *
   * Every limit of krpc::services::KRPC::hold_tick() applies, the timeout included. A hold lost to
   * the timeout is not reported, and the next call takes whatever tick it lands on.
   */
  void next_tick();

  /**
   * Let the game move on from the tick that krpc::services::KRPC::hold_tick() held it on. Does
   * nothing when no tick is held, which is the case once a hold has timed out.
   */
  void release_tick();

  /**
   * Remove a streaming request.
   *
   * Client libraries call this procedure to implement streams. Use the streams of your
   * client library instead. Calling this directly while using a client library has
   * undefined behavior.
   */
  void remove_stream(uint64_t id);

  /**
   * Run a server side function once, within a single physics tick, and return
   * the value it produces.
   *
   * Client libraries call this procedure to implement server side functions. Use the
   * server side functions of your client library instead. Calling this directly while
   * using a client library has undefined behavior.
   * @return The encoded value of the function, or no bytes for a function with no
   * value.
   * @param function The function to run.
   * @param arguments The encoded arguments, one per parameter.
   */
  std::optional<std::string> run_function(KRPC::Expression function, std::vector<std::optional<std::string> > arguments);

  /**
   * Set the update rate for a stream in Hz.
   *
   * Client libraries call this procedure to implement streams. Use the streams of your
   * client library instead. Calling this directly while using a client library has
   * undefined behavior.
   */
  void set_stream_rate(uint64_t id, float rate);

  /**
   * The clients that are currently connected to the server.
   */
  std::vector<KRPC::ClientInfo> clients();

  /**
   * The current game scene. Setting this switches the game to the given
   * scene, or opens/closes the corresponding facility for the pseudo-scenes.
   * Scene changes happen asynchronously: setting this property returns
   * immediately, and clients should poll it until it reports the requested
   * scene. Setting it to krpc::services::KRPC::GameScene::flight resumes the save's
   * active vessel, and fails if there is none. Moving between
   * krpc::services::KRPC::GameScene::editor_vab and krpc::services::KRPC::GameScene::editor_sph
   * keeps the vessel that is being constructed, as the game's own switch
   * editor button does. Leaving flight saves the game first, unless the game
   * cannot be saved, e.g. while under acceleration.
   */
  KRPC::GameScene game_scene();

  /**
   * The current game scene. Setting this switches the game to the given
   * scene, or opens/closes the corresponding facility for the pseudo-scenes.
   * Scene changes happen asynchronously: setting this property returns
   * immediately, and clients should poll it until it reports the requested
   * scene. Setting it to krpc::services::KRPC::GameScene::flight resumes the save's
   * active vessel, and fails if there is none. Moving between
   * krpc::services::KRPC::GameScene::editor_vab and krpc::services::KRPC::GameScene::editor_sph
   * keeps the vessel that is being constructed, as the game's own switch
   * editor button does. Leaving flight saves the game first, unless the game
   * cannot be saved, e.g. while under acceleration.
   */
  void set_game_scene(KRPC::GameScene value);

  /**
   * The number of game states that have finished loading.
   *
   * Increases once a game load, quickload, revert or scene change has finished.
   */
  uint32_t game_state_loads();

  /**
   * Whether the game is paused.
   */
  bool paused();

  /**
   * Whether the game is paused.
   */
  void set_paused(bool value);

  ::krpc::Stream<::krpc::Event> add_event_stream(KRPC::Expression function);

  ::krpc::Stream<krpc::schema::Stream> add_function_stream_stream(KRPC::Expression function);

  ::krpc::Stream<krpc::schema::Stream> add_stream_stream(krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call);

  ::krpc::Stream<std::string> get_client_id_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<std::string> get_client_name_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<krpc::schema::Services> get_services_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::ServerStatus> get_status_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<std::optional<std::string>> run_function_stream(KRPC::Expression function, std::vector<std::optional<std::string> > arguments);

  ::krpc::Stream<std::vector<KRPC::ClientInfo>> clients_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::GameScene> game_scene_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<uint32_t> game_state_loads_stream();

  ::krpc::Stream<bool> paused_stream();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall add_event_call(KRPC::Expression function);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall add_function_stream_call(KRPC::Expression function);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall add_stream_call(krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall compile_function_call(KRPC::Expression function);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall get_client_id_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall get_client_name_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall get_services_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall get_status_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall hold_tick_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall next_tick_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall release_tick_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall remove_stream_call(uint64_t id);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall run_function_call(KRPC::Expression function, std::vector<std::optional<std::string> > arguments);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall set_stream_rate_call(uint64_t id, float rate);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall clients_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall game_scene_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall set_game_scene_call(KRPC::GameScene value);

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall game_state_loads_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall paused_call();

  ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall set_paused_call(bool value);

  /**
   * A server side expression.
   *
   * Numeric operands of differing types are converted to a common type.
   */
  class Expression : public krpc::Object<Expression> {
   public:
    explicit Expression(Client* client = nullptr, uint64_t id = 0);

    /**
     * Numerical addition.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression add(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The result of applying an accumulator function over a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
     * @return The accumulated value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param func The accumulator function.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression aggregate(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    /**
     * The result of applying an accumulator function over a collection, starting from a seed.
     *
     * The seed widens to the type of the accumulated value, the function's result.
     * @return The accumulated value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param seed The seed value.
     * @param func The accumulator function.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression aggregate_with_seed(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression seed, KRPC::Expression func);

    /**
     * Whether a predicate function returns true for every value of a collection.
     * @return Whether all items satisfy the predicate.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param predicate The predicate function.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression all(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression predicate);

    /**
     * Logical or bitwise and, which evaluates both operands.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression and_(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Whether a predicate function returns true for any value of a collection.
     * @return Whether any item satisfies the predicate.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param predicate The predicate function.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression any(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression predicate);

    /**
     * A statement adding a value to a list or a set.
     *
     * The value goes on the end of a list. A set holding the value is left unchanged.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param collection The list or set to add to.
     * @param value The value to add.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression append(Client& client, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * An assignment to a local variable, state variable or function parameter.
     *
     * The value's type must be assignable to the variable's type. A numeric value of a
     * different type is widened, and narrowing it requires a cast.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param variable The variable to assign to.
     * @param value The value to assign.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression assign(Client& client, KRPC::Expression variable, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * The average of the values of a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty and its values are not nullable.
     * @return The average of the elements in the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression average(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A block of statements, whose value is that of its last statement.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param statements The statements.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression block(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> statements);

    /**
     * A block of statements with local variables.
     *
     * The value of the block is that of its last statement. The variables are in scope
     * for the statements of the block, including within nested functions.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param variables The local variables of the block.
     * @param statements The statements.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression block_with_variables(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> variables, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> statements);

    /**
     * A break statement, which ends the enclosing loop.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression break_(Client& client);

    /**
     * A dictionary built from a collection, with a key and a value computed from each
     * element.
     *
     * A repeated key takes the last of its values.
     * @return The dictionary.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     * @param key_func The function computing an element's key.
     * @param value_func The function computing an element's value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression build_dictionary(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key_func, KRPC::Expression value_func);

    /**
     * An RPC call.
     *
     * The instance the call is made on, and the values of its arguments, are fixed
     * when the expression is created. A call to a procedure with no return value is
     * evaluated for its effects, as a statement in a krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::block(). A collection the
     * procedure returns is copied, as a client would receive it.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param call The procedure call.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression call(Client& client, krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call);

    /**
     * An RPC call, where some or all of the arguments are computed by expressions.
     *
     * Position 0 is the instance of a class method or property. A position with no
     * expression takes the argument encoded in the call, or the parameter's default. A
     * collection the procedure returns is copied, as a client would receive it.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param call The RPC to call.
     * @param args Expressions computing the call's arguments, by position.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression call_with_arguments(Client& client, krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call, std::map<int32_t, KRPC::Expression> args);

    /**
     * A value cast to the given type.
     *
     * A tuple or collection is cast element by element.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The value to cast.
     * @param type Type to cast the argument to.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression cast(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Type type);

    /**
     * A statement removing every value from a list or a set, or every entry from a dictionary.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param collection The list, set or dictionary.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression clear(Client& client, KRPC::Expression collection);

    /**
     * The values of one collection followed by those of another.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The first collection followed by the second.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg1 The first collection.
     * @param arg2 The second collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression concat(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2);

    /**
     * The second argument if the condition is true, and the third argument otherwise.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the two values have no common type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param condition The boolean condition.
     * @param if_true The value when the condition is true.
     * @param if_false The value when the condition is false.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression conditional(Client& client, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression if_true, KRPC::Expression if_false);

    /**
     * Logical and, which evaluates the second operand only when the first is true.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression conditional_and(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Logical or, which evaluates the second operand only when the first is false.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression conditional_or(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * A constant value of boolean type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_bool(Client& client, bool value);

    /**
     * A constant value of bytes type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_bytes(Client& client, std::string value);

    /**
     * A constant value of double precision floating point type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_double(Client& client, double value);

    /**
     * A constant value of an enumeration a service defines.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param service The name of the service the enumeration is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the enumeration.
     * @param value The value of the member of the enumeration.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_enum(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name, int32_t value);

    /**
     * A constant value of single precision floating point type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_float(Client& client, float value);

    /**
     * A constant value of integer type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_int(Client& client, int32_t value);

    /**
     * A constant value of long integer type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_long(Client& client, int64_t value);

    /**
     * A null constant of the given type.
     *
     * The type is made nullable, as by krpc::services::KRPC::Type::nullable_type().
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param type The type of the value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_null(Client& client, KRPC::Type type);

    /**
     * A constant object, an instance of a class a service defines.
     *
     * The object is given by its object identifier.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The object identifier of the object.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_object(Client& client, uint64_t value);

    /**
     * A constant value of string type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_string(Client& client, std::string value);

    /**
     * A constant value of unsigned integer type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_u_int(Client& client, uint32_t value);

    /**
     * A constant value of unsigned long integer type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression constant_u_long(Client& client, uint64_t value);

    /**
     * Whether a collection contains a value.
     * @return Whether the collection contains the value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param value The value to look for.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression contains(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * Whether a dictionary contains a key.
     * @return Whether the dictionary contains the key.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param dictionary The dictionary.
     * @param key The key to look for.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression contains_key(Client& client, KRPC::Expression dictionary, KRPC::Expression key);

    /**
     * A continue statement, which skips to the next iteration of the enclosing loop.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression continue_(Client& client);

    /**
     * The string representation of a value.
     *
     * A value other than a number, boolean, string or enumeration converts as .NET
     * formats it, which for a collection is the name of its type. Throws an exception if
     * the value is null.
     * @return The string representation of the value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The value to convert.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression convert_to_string(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * The number of values in a collection.
     * @return The number of elements in the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list, set or dictionary.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression count(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A dictionary of the given keys and corresponding values, with numbers widened to
     * their common type. Throws an exception if there are no keys, the counts differ, the
     * keys are not of a valid key type, or a key is null.
     *
     * A repeated key takes the last of its values.
     * @return The dictionary.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param keys The keys.
     * @param values The values.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_dictionary(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> keys, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> values);

    /**
     * An empty dictionary of the given key and value types. Throws an exception if the
     * key type is nullable or not a valid key type.
     * @return The empty dictionary.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param key_type The type of the dictionary's keys.
     * @param value_type The type of the dictionary's values.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_empty_dictionary(Client& client, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * An empty list of the given value type.
     * @return The empty list.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value_type The type of the values the list holds.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_empty_list(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * An empty set of the given value type. Throws an exception if the value type is
     * nullable.
     * @return The empty set.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value_type The type of the values the set holds.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_empty_set(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A list of the given values, with numbers widened to their common type. Throws an
     * exception if there are no values.
     * @return The list.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param values The values.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_list(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> values);

    /**
     * A set of the given values, with numbers widened to their common type. Throws an
     * exception if there are no values, or a value is null.
     *
     * The values are evaluated in an unspecified order.
     * @return The set.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param values The values.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_set(Client& client, std::set<KRPC::Expression> values);

    /**
     * A structure with the given field values. Throws an exception if the field values do
     * not match its fields.
     * @return The structure.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param type The type of the structure.
     * @param field_values The values of the structure's fields,
     * in the order the structure declares them.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_struct(Client& client, KRPC::Type type, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> field_values);

    /**
     * A tuple of the given elements. Throws an exception if there are no elements or more
     * than seven.
     * @return The tuple.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param elements The elements.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression create_tuple(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> elements);

    /**
     * The keys of a dictionary, as a list.
     *
     * The collection operations take the keys of a dictionary from here, and its values
     * from krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::dictionary_values().
     * @return The keys of the dictionary.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param dictionary The dictionary.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression dictionary_keys(Client& client, KRPC::Expression dictionary);

    /**
     * The values of a dictionary, as a list.
     * @return The values of the dictionary.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param dictionary The dictionary.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression dictionary_values(Client& client, KRPC::Expression dictionary);

    /**
     * The values of a collection with duplicates removed, keeping the first
     * occurrence of each.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The distinct values of the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression distinct(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * Numerical division.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression divide(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The value at the given position in a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the index is out of range.
     * @return The value at the given position.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param index The position of the value, counting from zero.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression element_at(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index);

    /**
     * Equality comparison.
     *
     * Collections, tuples, bytes and structures compare by their contents. Throws an
     * exception if the operands are of unrelated types.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression equal(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The values of the first collection that are not in the second, with
     * duplicates removed.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The difference between the two collections.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg1 The first collection.
     * @param arg2 The second collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression except(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2);

    /**
     * Logical or bitwise exclusive or.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression exclusive_or(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The first value of a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
     * @return The first value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression first(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A loop that evaluates the body once for each value of a collection.
     *
     * The loop variable is a local variable declared by an enclosing
     * krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::block_with_variables(), or a parameter of an enclosing krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::lambda().
     * A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::break_() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::continue_() in the body applies to this loop.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param variable The loop variable.
     * @param collection The collection to iterate over.
     * @param body The statement evaluated on each iteration.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression for_each(Client& client, KRPC::Expression variable, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression body);

    /**
     * An element of a tuple, list or dictionary.
     *
     * A tuple takes a constant integer index, a list a zero indexed integer and a
     * dictionary a key. Throws an exception if the index is out of range or the key is
     * missing, and for a tuple when the expression is created.
     * @return The element.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The tuple, list or dictionary.
     * @param index The index or key of the element.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression get(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index);

    /**
     * The value of a field of a structure. Throws an exception if the value is not a
     * structure, or has no field of the given name.
     * @return The value of the field.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The structure.
     * @param name The name of the field to access.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression get_field(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, std::string name);

    /**
     * Greater than numerical comparison.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression greater_than(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Greater than or equal numerical comparison.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression greater_than_or_equal(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The values of a collection grouped by the key a function produces for each of them.
     * @return A dictionary of each key to the values that produced it.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param key A function producing the key to group values by.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression group_by(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key);

    /**
     * An if statement, with no value.
     *
     * Use krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::conditional() for an if-then-else that produces a value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param condition The boolean condition.
     * @param body The statement evaluated when the condition is true.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression if_then(Client& client, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body);

    /**
     * An if-else statement, with no value.
     *
     * Use krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::conditional() for an if-then-else that produces a value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param condition The boolean condition.
     * @param body The statement evaluated when the condition is true.
     * @param else_body The statement evaluated when the condition is false.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression if_then_else(Client& client, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body, KRPC::Expression else_body);

    /**
     * The values in both of two collections, with duplicates removed.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The intersection of the two collections.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg1 The first collection.
     * @param arg2 The second collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression intersect(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2);

    /**
     * A function call.
     * @return A function call.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param function The function to call.
     * @param args The arguments to call the function with.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression invoke(Client& client, KRPC::Expression function, std::map<std::string, KRPC::Expression> args);

    /**
     * Whether a value is null. Throws an exception if the type of the value cannot be null.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression is_null(Client& client, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * A function with the given parameters and body.
     *
     * The body is an expression or a block of statements, and a body with no value is
     * evaluated for its effects. A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::return_() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::return_nothing()
     * statement ends the evaluation.
     * @return A function.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param parameters The parameters of the function.
     * @param body The body of the function.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression lambda(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> parameters, KRPC::Expression body);

    /**
     * The last value of a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
     * @return The last value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression last(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * Bitwise left shift.
     *
     * Only the low 5 bits of the count are used for a 32-bit value, and the low 6 bits
     * for a 64-bit value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression left_shift(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Less than numerical comparison.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression less_than(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Less than or equal numerical comparison.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression less_than_or_equal(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The largest value of a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty and its values are not nullable.
     * @return The largest value in the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression max(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * The value of a collection with the largest key.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
     * @return The value with the largest key.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param key A function producing the key to compare values by.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression max_by(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key);

    /**
     * The smallest value of a collection.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty and its values are not nullable.
     * @return The smallest value in the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression min(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * The value of a collection with the smallest key.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the collection is empty.
     * @return The value with the smallest key.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param key A function producing the key to compare values by.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression min_by(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key);

    /**
     * Numerical modulo operator.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     * @return The remainder of arg0 divided by arg1
     */
    static KRPC::Expression modulo(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Numerical multiplication.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression multiply(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Numerical negation, which negates a uint as a long.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression negate(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * Logical or bitwise negation.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression not_(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * Inequality comparison.
     *
     * Collections, tuples, bytes and structures compare by their contents. Throws an
     * exception if the operands are of unrelated types.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression not_equal(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * Logical or bitwise or, which evaluates both operands.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression or_(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The values of a collection ordered by the key a function produces for each of them.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The ordered collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection to order.
     * @param key A function producing the key to order values by.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression order_by(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression key);

    /**
     * A named parameter of a function.
     *
     * A value is bound to it by name with krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::invoke(), or by its position in
     * the parameters of a krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::lambda().
     * @return A named parameter.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param name The name of the parameter.
     * @param type The type of the parameter.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression parameter(Client& client, std::string name, KRPC::Type type);

    /**
     * Numerical power operator.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     * @return arg0 raised to the power of arg1, at the common type of the operands
     */
    static KRPC::Expression power(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * A removal of a value from a list or a set, or of an entry from a dictionary.
     *
     * The first occurrence is removed from a list.
     * @return Whether the collection held the value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param collection The list, set or dictionary.
     * @param value The value to remove, or the key of the entry to
     * remove.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression remove(Client& client, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * A statement removing the element at an index of a list.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the index is out of range.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param list The list.
     * @param index The index of the element, counting from zero.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression remove_at(Client& client, KRPC::Expression list, KRPC::Expression index);

    /**
     * A return statement, which ends the enclosing function with a value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value The value to return.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression return_(Client& client, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * A return statement, which ends the enclosing function with no value.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression return_nothing(Client& client);

    /**
     * The values of a collection in the opposite order.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The reversed collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression reverse(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * Bitwise right shift.
     *
     * Only the low 5 bits of the count are used for a 32-bit value, and the low 6 bits
     * for a 64-bit value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression right_shift(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The result of a function for each value of a collection.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The collection of function results.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     * @param func The function, taking an element of the collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression select(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    /**
     * The values of the collections a function returns for each value of a collection,
     * flattened into one.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The flattened collection of function results.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     * @param func The function, taking an element of the collection
     * and returning a collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression select_many(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    /**
     * A statement setting an element of a list, or an entry of a dictionary.
     *
     * A key the dictionary does not hold is added. Throws an exception if the index
     * is out of range for the list.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param collection The list or dictionary.
     * @param index The index of the element, counting from zero, or the key of the entry.
     * @param value The value to set.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression set(Client& client, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression index, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * A collection without its first count values.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The collection without its first count values.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param count The number of values to skip.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression skip(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression count);

    /**
     * A variable that keeps its value between evaluations of a function.
     *
     * The initial value is evaluated once, when the variable is created, and gives the
     * variable its type. The value belongs to the variable, so every function that
     * contains it shares it.
     * @return A state variable.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param name The name of the variable.
     * @param initial_value The initial value of the variable.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression state_variable(Client& client, std::string name, KRPC::Expression initial_value);

    /**
     * The concatenation of strings.
     *
     * Use krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::convert_to_string() to convert other values to strings.
     * @return The concatenated string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param args The strings to concatenate, in order.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_concat(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> args);

    /**
     * Whether one string occurs within another.
     * @return Whether the value occurs in the string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string to search.
     * @param value The string to search for.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_contains(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * Whether a string ends with another.
     * @return Whether the string ends with the value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string to test.
     * @param value The string to test for.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_ends_with(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * The character at the given position in a string, as a string of length one.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the index is out of range.
     * @return The character at the given position.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     * @param index The position of the character, counting from zero.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_get(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index);

    /**
     * The position of the first occurrence of one string within another.
     * @return The position of the value, counting from zero, or -1 if it does not occur.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string to search.
     * @param value The string to search for.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_index_of(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * Strings joined together, separated by another string.
     * @return The joined string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param separator The string to put between the values.
     * @param values The strings to join.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_join(Client& client, KRPC::Expression separator, KRPC::Expression values);

    /**
     * The number of characters in a string.
     * @return The length of the string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_length(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A string with every occurrence of one string replaced by another.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the string to replace is empty.
     * @return The string with the replacements made.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string to search.
     * @param old_value The string to replace.
     * @param new_value The string to replace it with.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_replace(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression old_value, KRPC::Expression new_value);

    /**
     * The parts of a string separated by another string.
     * @return The parts of the string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string to split.
     * @param separator The string that separates the parts.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_split(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression separator);

    /**
     * Whether a string starts with another.
     * @return Whether the string starts with the value.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string to test.
     * @param value The string to test for.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_starts_with(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression value);

    /**
     * Part of a string.
     *
     * Throws an exception if the start or the length is out of range.
     * @return The substring.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     * @param start The position to start at, counting from zero.
     * @param length The number of characters to take.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_substring(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression start, KRPC::Expression length);

    /**
     * A string in lower case, independently of the game's language.
     * @return The string in lower case.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_to_lower(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A string in upper case, independently of the game's language.
     * @return The string in upper case.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_to_upper(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A string with white space removed from both ends.
     * @return The trimmed string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_trim(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A string with white space removed from its end.
     * @return The trimmed string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_trim_end(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A string with white space removed from its start.
     * @return The trimmed string.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The string.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression string_trim_start(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * Numerical subtraction.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg0 The first operand.
     * @param arg1 The second operand.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression subtract(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    /**
     * The sum of the values of a collection.
     * @return The sum of the elements in the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression sum(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * The first count values of a collection.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The first count values of the collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     * @param count The number of values to take.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression take(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression count);

    /**
     * A throw statement, which raises the named exception.
     *
     * The client receives the exception as an error from the function, or from the stream
     * or event evaluating it. For example, the service `KRPC` and the name
     * `Error` raise krpc::services::KRPC::Error. Throws an exception if the service does not
     * declare it.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param service The name of the service the exception is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the exception.
     * @param message The message of the exception.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression throw_(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name, KRPC::Expression message);

    /**
     * A collection as a list.
     * @return The collection as a list.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression to_list(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A collection as a set.
     *
     * Throws an exception if a value is null.
     * @return The collection as a set.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression to_set(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    /**
     * A try-catch statement that handles the named exception.
     *
     * Only an exception a service declares can be named, and krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::try_catch_all()
     * handles any exception. Throws an exception if the service does not declare it.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param body The statement to evaluate.
     * @param service The name of the service the exception is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the exception.
     * @param message A string variable assigned the exception's message, or null.
     * @param handler The statement evaluated when the exception is caught.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression try_catch(Client& client, KRPC::Expression body, std::string service, std::string name, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler);

    /**
     * A try-catch statement that handles any exception.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param body The statement to evaluate.
     * @param message A string variable assigned the exception's message, or null.
     * @param handler The statement evaluated when an exception is caught.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression try_catch_all(Client& client, KRPC::Expression body, std::optional<KRPC::Expression> message, KRPC::Expression handler);

    /**
     * A try-finally statement.
     *
     * The finalizer is evaluated after the body, whether or not the body raises an
     * exception. A break, continue or return cannot leave the finalizer.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param body The statement to evaluate.
     * @param finalizer The statement evaluated after the body.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression try_finally(Client& client, KRPC::Expression body, KRPC::Expression finalizer);

    /**
     * The values in either of two collections, with duplicates removed.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The union of the two collections.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg1 The first collection.
     * @param arg2 The second collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression union_(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2);

    /**
     * A local variable, declared by krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::block_with_variables().
     * @return A local variable.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param name The name of the variable.
     * @param type The type of the variable.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression variable(Client& client, std::string name, KRPC::Type type);

    /**
     * The values of a collection for which a predicate function returns true.
     *
     * The result is lazily evaluated. krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The filtered collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg The list or set.
     * @param func The predicate function, taking an element of the collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression where(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    /**
     * A while loop, which evaluates the body while the condition is true.
     *
     * A krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::break_() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::continue_() in the body applies to this loop.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param condition The boolean condition.
     * @param body The statement evaluated on each iteration.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression while_(Client& client, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body);

    /**
     * The results of a function applied to the values of two collections in pairs.
     *
     * The result is as long as the shorter of the two collections. It is lazily
     * evaluated, and krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() makes it concrete.
     * @return The combined collection.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param arg1 The first collection.
     * @param arg2 The second collection.
     * @param func A function combining a value from each collection.
     */
    static KRPC::Expression zip(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2, KRPC::Expression func);

    /**
     * Whether the expression evaluates to a value.
     *
     * An expression with no value has no krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::return_type(). An empty
     * collection and no value both encode to an empty sequence of bytes.
     */
    bool has_return_type();

    /**
     * The types of the function's parameters.
     *
     * Empty for a function that is not created with krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::lambda().
     */
    std::vector<KRPC::Type> parameter_types();

    /**
     * The type of the value the expression evaluates to. Throws an exception if the
     * value cannot be sent to a client.
     *
     * A function created with krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::lambda() evaluates to the value it returns. A
     * lazily evaluated collection, such as from krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::select(), is sent once
     * converted by krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_list() or krpc::services::KRPC::Expression::to_set().
     */
    KRPC::Type return_type();

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall constant_u_long_call(Client& client, uint64_t value);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_dictionary_call(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> keys, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> values);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_empty_dictionary_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_empty_list_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_empty_set_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_list_call(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> values);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_struct_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type type, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> field_values);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall create_tuple_call(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Expression> elements);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall get_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression index);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall get_field_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall greater_than_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall if_then_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall last_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall left_shift_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall less_than_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall max_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall parameter_call(Client& client, std::string name, KRPC::Type type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall power_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall remove_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression value);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall remove_at_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression list, KRPC::Expression index);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall return__call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression value);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall reverse_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall right_shift_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg0, KRPC::Expression arg1);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall select_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall select_many_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression func);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall set_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression collection, KRPC::Expression index, KRPC::Expression value);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall skip_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg, KRPC::Expression count);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall state_variable_call(Client& client, std::string name, KRPC::Expression initial_value);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall to_list_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall union__call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall variable_call(Client& client, std::string name, KRPC::Type type);
