    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall while__call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression condition, KRPC::Expression body);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall zip_call(Client& client, KRPC::Expression arg1, KRPC::Expression arg2, KRPC::Expression func);

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall has_return_type_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall parameter_types_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall return_type_call();
  };

  /**
   * The type of a value a procedure takes or returns.
   */
  class Type : public krpc::Object<Type> {
   public:
    explicit Type(Client* client = nullptr, uint64_t id = 0);

    /**
     * A boolean type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type bool_(Client& client);

    /**
     * A byte sequence type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type bytes(Client& client);

    /**
     * The type of a class defined by a service.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param service The name of the service the class is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the class.
     */
    static KRPC::Type class_type(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    /**
     * A dictionary type. Throws an exception if the key type is nullable or not a valid
     * key type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param key_type The type of the keys of the dictionary.
     * @param value_type The type of the values of the dictionary.
     */
    static KRPC::Type dictionary_type(Client& client, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A double precision floating point number type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type double_(Client& client);

    /**
     * The type of an enumeration defined by a service.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param service The name of the service the enumeration is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the enumeration.
     */
    static KRPC::Type enumeration_type(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    /**
     * A single precision floating point number type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type float_(Client& client);

    /**
     * A signed 32-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type int_(Client& client);

    /**
     * A list type.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value_type The type of the elements of the list.
     */
    static KRPC::Type list_type(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A signed 64-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type long_(Client& client);

    /**
     * A nullable type, holding a value of the given type or null.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value_type The type of the value.
     */
    static KRPC::Type nullable_type(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A set type. Throws an exception if the element type is nullable.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value_type The type of the elements of the set.
     */
    static KRPC::Type set_type(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    /**
     * A string type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type string(Client& client);

    /**
     * The type of a structure defined by a service.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param service The name of the service the structure is defined in.
     * @param name The name of the structure.
     */
    static KRPC::Type struct_type(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    /**
     * A tuple type. Throws an exception if there are no element types or more than seven.
     * @param client A client object.
     * @param value_types The types of the elements of the tuple.
     */
    static KRPC::Type tuple_type(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types);

    /**
     * An unsigned 32-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type u_int(Client& client);

    /**
     * An unsigned 64-bit integer type.
     */
    static KRPC::Type u_long(Client& client);

    /**
     * The kind of the type.
     */
    KRPC::TypeCode code();

    /**
     * The name of a class, enumeration or structure type, or an empty string.
     */
    std::string name();

    /**
     * Whether a value of the type may be null.
     *
     * Any type can be nullable. A string or class value of a type that is not nullable
     * is never null.
     */
    bool nullable();

    /**
     * The service a class, enumeration or structure type is defined in, or an empty string.
     */
    std::string service();

    /**
     * The types of the elements of a tuple, list, set or dictionary type.
     *
     * The value types of a tuple, the value type of a list or set, or the key and value
     * types of a dictionary. An empty list for any other type.
     */
    std::vector<KRPC::Type> types();

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> bool__stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> bytes_stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> class_type_stream(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> dictionary_type_stream(Client& client, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> double__stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> enumeration_type_stream(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> float__stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> int__stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> list_type_stream(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> long__stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> nullable_type_stream(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> set_type_stream(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> string_stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> struct_type_stream(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> tuple_type_stream(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> u_int_stream(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type> u_long_stream(Client& client);

    ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::TypeCode> code_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<std::string> name_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<bool> nullable_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<std::string> service_stream();

    ::krpc::Stream<std::vector<KRPC::Type>> types_stream();

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall bool__call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall bytes_call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall class_type_call(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall dictionary_type_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type key_type, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall double__call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall enumeration_type_call(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall float__call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall int__call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall list_type_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall long__call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall nullable_type_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall set_type_call(Client& client, KRPC::Type value_type);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall string_call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall struct_type_call(Client& client, std::string service, std::string name);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall tuple_type_call(Client& client, std::vector<KRPC::Type> value_types);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall u_int_call(Client& client);

    static ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall u_long_call(Client& client);

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall code_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall name_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall nullable_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall service_call();

    ::krpc::schema::ProcedureCall types_call();
  };

  /**
   * A client connected to the server.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::clients().
   */
  struct ClientInfo {
    /**
     * The identifier of the client.
     */
    std::string id;
    /**
     * The name of the client, or an empty string if it has no name.
     */
    std::string name;
    /**
     * The address of the client.
     */
    std::string address;
    /**
     * The protocol version of the client's connection.
     */
    uint32_t protocol_version;

    ClientInfo() : id(), name(), address(), protocol_version() {}

    ClientInfo(std::string id, std::string name, std::string address, uint32_t protocol_version) :
id(id), name(name), address(address), protocol_version(protocol_version) {}

    bool operator==(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return this->id == other.id && this->name == other.name && this->address == other.address && this->protocol_version == other.protocol_version;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      if (id < other.id)
        return true;
      if (other.id < id)
        return false;
      if (name < other.name)
        return true;
      if (other.name < name)
        return false;
      if (address < other.address)
        return true;
      if (other.address < address)
        return false;
      if (protocol_version < other.protocol_version)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ClientInfo& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * The settings that control how the server executes calls.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus::settings.
   */
  struct ServerSettings {
    /**
     * Whether the server executes one call per update.
     */
    bool one_rpc_per_update;
    /**
     * The maximum time spent executing calls per update, in microseconds.
     */
    uint32_t max_time_per_update;
    /**
     * Whether the server adjusts krpc::services::KRPC::ServerSettings::max_time_per_update to hold the frame rate.
     */
    bool adaptive_rate_control;
    /**
     * Whether the server waits for the next call in an update.
     */
    bool blocking_recv;
    /**
     * The time the server waits for the next call, in microseconds.
     */
    uint32_t recv_timeout;
    /**
     * The longest time a client may hold the game on one physics tick, in microseconds.
     *
     * See krpc::services::KRPC::hold_tick().
     */
    uint32_t tick_hold_timeout;

    ServerSettings() : one_rpc_per_update(), max_time_per_update(), adaptive_rate_control(), blocking_recv(), recv_timeout(), tick_hold_timeout() {}

    ServerSettings(bool one_rpc_per_update, uint32_t max_time_per_update, bool adaptive_rate_control, bool blocking_recv, uint32_t recv_timeout, uint32_t tick_hold_timeout) :
one_rpc_per_update(one_rpc_per_update), max_time_per_update(max_time_per_update), adaptive_rate_control(adaptive_rate_control), blocking_recv(blocking_recv), recv_timeout(recv_timeout), tick_hold_timeout(tick_hold_timeout) {}

    bool operator==(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return this->one_rpc_per_update == other.one_rpc_per_update && this->max_time_per_update == other.max_time_per_update && this->adaptive_rate_control == other.adaptive_rate_control && this->blocking_recv == other.blocking_recv && this->recv_timeout == other.recv_timeout && this->tick_hold_timeout == other.tick_hold_timeout;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      if (one_rpc_per_update < other.one_rpc_per_update)
        return true;
      if (other.one_rpc_per_update < one_rpc_per_update)
        return false;
      if (max_time_per_update < other.max_time_per_update)
        return true;
      if (other.max_time_per_update < max_time_per_update)
        return false;
      if (adaptive_rate_control < other.adaptive_rate_control)
        return true;
      if (other.adaptive_rate_control < adaptive_rate_control)
        return false;
      if (blocking_recv < other.blocking_recv)
        return true;
      if (other.blocking_recv < blocking_recv)
        return false;
      if (recv_timeout < other.recv_timeout)
        return true;
      if (other.recv_timeout < recv_timeout)
        return false;
      if (tick_hold_timeout < other.tick_hold_timeout)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ServerSettings& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * The performance statistics of the server.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus::statistics.
   */
  struct ServerStatistics {
    /**
     * The number of bytes received from clients.
     */
    uint64_t bytes_read;
    /**
     * The number of bytes sent to clients.
     */
    uint64_t bytes_written;
    /**
     * The rate bytes are received from clients, in bytes per second.
     */
    float bytes_read_rate;
    /**
     * The rate bytes are sent to clients, in bytes per second.
     */
    float bytes_written_rate;
    /**
     * The number of calls executed.
     */
    uint64_t rpcs_executed;
    /**
     * The number of calls executed per second.
     */
    float rpc_rate;
    /**
     * The time spent receiving and executing calls per update, in seconds.
     */
    float time_per_rpc_update;
    /**
     * The time spent receiving calls per update, in seconds.
     */
    float poll_time_per_rpc_update;
    /**
     * The time spent executing calls per update, in seconds.
     */
    float exec_time_per_rpc_update;
    /**
     * The number of calls left for the next update, because the update ran out of time
     * or the call yielded.
     */
    uint32_t deferred_calls;
    /**
     * The number of open streams, events included.
     */
    uint32_t streams;
    /**
     * The number of stream evaluations, one per stream per update.
     */
    uint64_t stream_evaluations;
    /**
     * The number of stream evaluations per second.
     */
    float stream_evaluation_rate;
    /**
     * The number of stream updates sent to clients.
     */
    uint64_t stream_updates_sent;
    /**
     * The time spent updating streams per update, in seconds.
     */
    float time_per_stream_update;

    ServerStatistics() : bytes_read(), bytes_written(), bytes_read_rate(), bytes_written_rate(), rpcs_executed(), rpc_rate(), time_per_rpc_update(), poll_time_per_rpc_update(), exec_time_per_rpc_update(), deferred_calls(), streams(), stream_evaluations(), stream_evaluation_rate(), stream_updates_sent(), time_per_stream_update() {}

    ServerStatistics(uint64_t bytes_read, uint64_t bytes_written, float bytes_read_rate, float bytes_written_rate, uint64_t rpcs_executed, float rpc_rate, float time_per_rpc_update, float poll_time_per_rpc_update, float exec_time_per_rpc_update, uint32_t deferred_calls, uint32_t streams, uint64_t stream_evaluations, float stream_evaluation_rate, uint64_t stream_updates_sent, float time_per_stream_update) :
bytes_read(bytes_read), bytes_written(bytes_written), bytes_read_rate(bytes_read_rate), bytes_written_rate(bytes_written_rate), rpcs_executed(rpcs_executed), rpc_rate(rpc_rate), time_per_rpc_update(time_per_rpc_update), poll_time_per_rpc_update(poll_time_per_rpc_update), exec_time_per_rpc_update(exec_time_per_rpc_update), deferred_calls(deferred_calls), streams(streams), stream_evaluations(stream_evaluations), stream_evaluation_rate(stream_evaluation_rate), stream_updates_sent(stream_updates_sent), time_per_stream_update(time_per_stream_update) {}

    bool operator==(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return this->bytes_read == other.bytes_read && this->bytes_written == other.bytes_written && this->bytes_read_rate == other.bytes_read_rate && this->bytes_written_rate == other.bytes_written_rate && this->rpcs_executed == other.rpcs_executed && this->rpc_rate == other.rpc_rate && this->time_per_rpc_update == other.time_per_rpc_update && this->poll_time_per_rpc_update == other.poll_time_per_rpc_update && this->exec_time_per_rpc_update == other.exec_time_per_rpc_update && this->deferred_calls == other.deferred_calls && this->streams == other.streams && this->stream_evaluations == other.stream_evaluations && this->stream_evaluation_rate == other.stream_evaluation_rate && this->stream_updates_sent == other.stream_updates_sent && this->time_per_stream_update == other.time_per_stream_update;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      if (bytes_read < other.bytes_read)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_read < bytes_read)
        return false;
      if (bytes_written < other.bytes_written)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_written < bytes_written)
        return false;
      if (bytes_read_rate < other.bytes_read_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_read_rate < bytes_read_rate)
        return false;
      if (bytes_written_rate < other.bytes_written_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.bytes_written_rate < bytes_written_rate)
        return false;
      if (rpcs_executed < other.rpcs_executed)
        return true;
      if (other.rpcs_executed < rpcs_executed)
        return false;
      if (rpc_rate < other.rpc_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.rpc_rate < rpc_rate)
        return false;
      if (time_per_rpc_update < other.time_per_rpc_update)
        return true;
      if (other.time_per_rpc_update < time_per_rpc_update)
        return false;
      if (poll_time_per_rpc_update < other.poll_time_per_rpc_update)
        return true;
      if (other.poll_time_per_rpc_update < poll_time_per_rpc_update)
        return false;
      if (exec_time_per_rpc_update < other.exec_time_per_rpc_update)
        return true;
      if (other.exec_time_per_rpc_update < exec_time_per_rpc_update)
        return false;
      if (deferred_calls < other.deferred_calls)
        return true;
      if (other.deferred_calls < deferred_calls)
        return false;
      if (streams < other.streams)
        return true;
      if (other.streams < streams)
        return false;
      if (stream_evaluations < other.stream_evaluations)
        return true;
      if (other.stream_evaluations < stream_evaluations)
        return false;
      if (stream_evaluation_rate < other.stream_evaluation_rate)
        return true;
      if (other.stream_evaluation_rate < stream_evaluation_rate)
        return false;
      if (stream_updates_sent < other.stream_updates_sent)
        return true;
      if (other.stream_updates_sent < stream_updates_sent)
        return false;
      if (time_per_stream_update < other.time_per_stream_update)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ServerStatistics& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * The status of the server.
   *
   * See krpc::services::KRPC::get_status().
   */
  struct ServerStatus {
    /**
     * The version of the server, or `unknown`.
     */
    std::string version;
    /**
     * The protocol version of the connection.
     */
    uint32_t protocol_version;
    /**
     * The settings of the server.
     */
    KRPC::ServerSettings settings;
    /**
     * The performance statistics of the server.
     */
    KRPC::ServerStatistics statistics;

    ServerStatus() : version(), protocol_version(), settings(), statistics() {}

    ServerStatus(std::string version, uint32_t protocol_version, KRPC::ServerSettings settings, KRPC::ServerStatistics statistics) :
version(version), protocol_version(protocol_version), settings(settings), statistics(statistics) {}

    bool operator==(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return this->version == other.version && this->protocol_version == other.protocol_version && this->settings == other.settings && this->statistics == other.statistics;
    }

    bool operator!=(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      if (version < other.version)
        return true;
      if (other.version < version)
        return false;
      if (protocol_version < other.protocol_version)
        return true;
      if (other.protocol_version < protocol_version)
        return false;
      if (settings < other.settings)
        return true;
      if (other.settings < settings)
        return false;
      if (statistics < other.statistics)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const ServerStatus& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };
};

}  // namespace services

namespace encoder {

  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::GameScene& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::TypeCode& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.id));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.name));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.address));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.protocol_version));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.one_rpc_per_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.max_time_per_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.adaptive_rate_control));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.blocking_recv));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.recv_timeout));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.tick_hold_timeout));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_read));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_written));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_read_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bytes_written_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.rpcs_executed));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.rpc_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.time_per_rpc_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.poll_time_per_rpc_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.exec_time_per_rpc_update));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.deferred_calls));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.streams));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.stream_evaluations));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.stream_evaluation_rate));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.stream_updates_sent));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.time_per_stream_update));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.version));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.protocol_version));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.settings));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.statistics));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

}  // namespace encoder

namespace decoder {

  inline void decode(services::KRPC::GameScene& value, const std::string& data, Client* client) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, client);
    value = static_cast<services::KRPC::GameScene>(x);
  }

  inline void decode(services::KRPC::TypeCode& value, const std::string& data, Client* client) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, client);
    value = static_cast<services::KRPC::TypeCode>(x);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value, const std::string& data, Client* client) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 4)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ClientInfo does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.id, structMessage.items(0), client);
    decode_item(value.name, structMessage.items(1), client);
    decode_item(value.address, structMessage.items(2), client);
    decode_item(value.protocol_version, structMessage.items(3), client);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value, const std::string& data, Client* client) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 6)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ServerSettings does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.one_rpc_per_update, structMessage.items(0), client);
    decode_item(value.max_time_per_update, structMessage.items(1), client);
    decode_item(value.adaptive_rate_control, structMessage.items(2), client);
    decode_item(value.blocking_recv, structMessage.items(3), client);
    decode_item(value.recv_timeout, structMessage.items(4), client);
    decode_item(value.tick_hold_timeout, structMessage.items(5), client);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value, const std::string& data, Client* client) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 15)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ServerStatistics does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.bytes_read, structMessage.items(0), client);
    decode_item(value.bytes_written, structMessage.items(1), client);
    decode_item(value.bytes_read_rate, structMessage.items(2), client);
    decode_item(value.bytes_written_rate, structMessage.items(3), client);
    decode_item(value.rpcs_executed, structMessage.items(4), client);
    decode_item(value.rpc_rate, structMessage.items(5), client);
    decode_item(value.time_per_rpc_update, structMessage.items(6), client);
    decode_item(value.poll_time_per_rpc_update, structMessage.items(7), client);
    decode_item(value.exec_time_per_rpc_update, structMessage.items(8), client);
    decode_item(value.deferred_calls, structMessage.items(9), client);
    decode_item(value.streams, structMessage.items(10), client);
    decode_item(value.stream_evaluations, structMessage.items(11), client);
    decode_item(value.stream_evaluation_rate, structMessage.items(12), client);
    decode_item(value.stream_updates_sent, structMessage.items(13), client);
    decode_item(value.time_per_stream_update, structMessage.items(14), client);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value, const std::string& data, Client* client) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 4)
      throw EncodingError("Value for ServerStatus does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.version, structMessage.items(0), client);
    decode_item(value.protocol_version, structMessage.items(1), client);
    decode_item(value.settings, structMessage.items(2), client);
    decode_item(value.statistics, structMessage.items(3), client);
  }

}  // namespace decoder

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.id));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.name));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.address));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.protocol_version));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.one_rpc_per_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.max_time_per_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.adaptive_rate_control));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.blocking_recv));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.recv_timeout));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.tick_hold_timeout));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_read));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_written));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_read_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bytes_written_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.rpcs_executed));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.rpc_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.time_per_rpc_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.poll_time_per_rpc_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.exec_time_per_rpc_update));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.deferred_calls));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.streams));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.stream_evaluations));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.stream_evaluation_rate));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.stream_updates_sent));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.time_per_stream_update));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.version));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.protocol_version));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.settings));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.statistics));
  return seed;
}

// A collection encoder or decoder calls encode and decode on the type it contains
// without qualifying them. The ones above are declared after those templates, so
// argument dependent lookup is what reaches them. It searches the namespace that
// encloses the type, which is this one. These name the same functions here, as a
// second function with the same signature is ambiguous to a compiler that finds both.
namespace services {
  using krpc::encoder::encode;
  using krpc::decoder::decode;
  using krpc::hash_value;
}  // namespace services

namespace services {

inline KRPC::KRPC(Client* client):
  Service(client) {
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ArgumentException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ArgumentException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ArgumentNullException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ArgumentNullException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ArgumentOutOfRangeException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ArgumentOutOfRangeException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "DivideByZeroException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw DivideByZeroException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "Error",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw Error(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "IndexOutOfRangeException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw IndexOutOfRangeException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "InvalidOperationException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw InvalidOperationException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "KeyNotFoundException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw KeyNotFoundException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "NotSupportedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw NotSupportedException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "NullReferenceException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw NullReferenceException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ObjectDestroyedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ObjectDestroyedException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "ObjectNotLoadedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw ObjectNotLoadedException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "OverflowException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw OverflowException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "StreamRemovedException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw StreamRemovedException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "TimeoutException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw TimeoutException(msg); });
  client->add_exception_thrower(
    "KRPC", "UnsupportedSceneException",
    [] (const std::string& msg) { throw UnsupportedSceneException(msg); });
}

inline ::krpc::Event KRPC::add_event(KRPC::Expression function) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(function));
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "AddEvent", _args, ::krpc::Client::Returns::event);
  ::krpc::Event _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline krpc::schema::Stream KRPC::add_function_stream(KRPC::Expression function) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(function));
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "AddFunctionStream", _args, ::krpc::Client::Returns::stream);
  krpc::schema::Stream _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline krpc::schema::Stream KRPC::add_stream(krpc::schema::ProcedureCall call) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(call));
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "AddStream", _args, ::krpc::Client::Returns::stream);
  krpc::schema::Stream _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline void KRPC::compile_function(KRPC::Expression function) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(function));
  this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "CompileFunction", _args, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
}

inline std::string KRPC::get_client_id() {
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "GetClientID", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
  std::string _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline std::string KRPC::get_client_name() {
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "GetClientName", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
  std::string _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline krpc::schema::Services KRPC::get_services() {
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "GetServices", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
  krpc::schema::Services _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline KRPC::ServerStatus KRPC::get_status() {
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "GetStatus", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
  KRPC::ServerStatus _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;
}

inline void KRPC::hold_tick() {
  this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "HoldTick", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
}

inline void KRPC::next_tick() {
  this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "NextTick", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
}

inline void KRPC::release_tick() {
  this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "ReleaseTick", {}, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
}

inline void KRPC::remove_stream(uint64_t id) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(1);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(id));
  this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "RemoveStream", _args, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
}

inline std::optional<std::string> KRPC::run_function(KRPC::Expression function, std::vector<std::optional<std::string> > arguments = std::vector<std::optional<std::string>>{}) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(2);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(function));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(arguments));
  auto _data = this->_client->invoke("KRPC", "RunFunction", _args, ::krpc::Client::Returns::value);
  std::optional<std::string> _result{};
  if (!_data.is_null())
    decoder::decode(_result, _data.value(), this->_client);
  return _result;

inline ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Expression> KRPC::Expression::string_join_stream(Client& _client, KRPC::Expression separator, KRPC::Expression values) {
  std::vector<krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(2);
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(separator));
  _args.push_back(encoder::encode_arg(values));
  return ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Expression>(&_client, _client.build_call("KRPC", "Expression_static_StringJoin", _args));
}

  _args.reserve(2);

  return ::krpc::Stream<KRPC::Type>(&_client, _client.build_call("KRPC", "Type_static_Long"));
}

// A structure is a type of this program's own, so unlike a tuple it can be given a std::hash,
// and an unordered container of one needs nothing further.
namespace std {

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ClientInfo> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ClientInfo& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ServerSettings> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ServerSettings& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatistics> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatistics& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

template <>
struct hash<krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus> {
  std::size_t operator()(const krpc::services::KRPC::ServerStatus& value) const {
    return krpc::hash_value(value);
  }
};

}  // namespace std
